## EBPM:EBPM-071

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-008.md#EBPM-EBPM-071">EBPM:EBPM-071</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-071</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Coupled revision of representations and work</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_COMPOSITION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>COMPOSITION</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S004</p></li><li><p>S005</p></li><li><p>S025</p></li><li><p>S027</p></li><li><p>S041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. Review their consistency and continuity: updating a diagram does not change work, and changed work may require a revised account without implying improvement.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Consequences or exceptions challenge the current process account or a revision is proposed.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A local correction to a note may suffice when work and authority are unchanged; avoid reopening the entire portfolio for a representational typo.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Identified mismatch, actors authorised for the relevant kind of change and evidence of affected dependencies.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/f8.md#r-f8f1c622a7a10b3993e6e88dea3b2d6efc07757703bfd6e34f2c5ef2cffea866">Complete shared assumptions · f8f1c622a7a1</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Modellers may revise descriptions within their remit; changing operational commitments or evaluation criteria requires the responsible authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Tell performers and consumers what changed in work, representation, objectives or evidence and what remains unchanged.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/bb.md#r-bb71b2227ba0324704b9655de596f91edea6b35f9ea3022b15fd5e6bc2aa9a10">Complete shared consumer · bb71b2227ba0</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>The revision’s type and operational consequences are explicit, with critical outstanding obligations preserved.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Model laundering of bad practice; uncommunicated process change; evaluation criteria rewritten to declare success; loss of continuity.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Model laundering of bad practice; uncommunicated process change; evaluation criteria rewritten to declare success; loss of continuity.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>This synthesis is not a claim that BPM methods are automatically self-authorising or that feedback converges to a globally optimal process.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S004">EBPM:S004</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S005">EBPM:S005</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S025">EBPM:S025</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S027">EBPM:S027</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S041">EBPM:S041</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-006.md#EBPM-S054">EBPM:S054</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-006.md#EBPM-S056">EBPM:S056</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R010</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R032</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R042</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R047</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R051</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>A local correction to a note may suffice when work and authority are unchanged; ## EBPM:EBPM-072

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-008.md#EBPM-EBPM-072">EBPM:EBPM-072</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-072</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Governed low-code participation and delivery</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>RESEARCH_EXTRACTION_AND_CRITICISM_ADJUDICATION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>B8</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S044</p></li><li><p>S045</p></li><li><p>S046</p></li><li><p>S047</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. Distinguish a simpler modelling interface from validated competence to change shared processes or sensitive data use.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Non-specialists or decentralised teams build applications that affect process operation.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Use a supported template or existing application; professional development may dominate where risk or integration complexity exceeds local capability.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Task-fit language, training/support, bounded data/system access and accountable review of consequential delivery.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/21.md#r-21ec0205c1e8ba08555ee017b267b3819657f78fd29645927562f02c46e94d72">Complete shared assumptions · 21ec0205c1e8</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>A citizen developer may create within delegated scope; do not equate terminal status with success.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Parallel work, exceptions, optional/discretionary items or external actions make completion ambiguous.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A directly inspected result and an agreed completion criterion may replace a formal end-state model.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/5f.md#r-5f19b651d5acf466c1dd7859f44ac365b176162893fdfbbce68705c8f62d9cc4">Complete shared prerequisites required input · 5f19b651d5ac</a></p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/40.md#r-40a926dccd07e134c1f3175653d8306972c5595f8db7cb2eff6a318d9074658f">Complete shared assumptions · 40a926dccd07</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>A permitted engine completion is not authority to dismiss somebody else’s unresolved obligation.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Communicate the completion claim and its exclusions, including any approved transfer of remaining work.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Case owner, requester/recipient and engine completion evaluator.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>No unaccounted active work remains under the chosen scope; the result claim states what was and was not established.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Premature closure, abandoned work, or a successful-looking case whose required effect did not occur.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Premature closure, abandoned work, or a successful-looking case whose required effect did not occur.</p></li><li><p>Overly strong completion rules can keep legitimate cases open indefinitely; form</dt><dd><p>Completion is specified by final markings, explicit termination or case/stage lifecycle rules.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>Parallel work, exceptions, optional/discretionary items or external actions make completion ambiguous. The consequential failure is Premature closure, abandoned work, or a successful-looking case whose required effect did not occur.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>Premature closure, abandoned work, or a successful-looking case whose required effect did not occur.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Declare what instance completion establishes, what work may remain and whether residual effects are closed, transferred or explicitly accepted; do not equate terminal status with success.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>Parallel work, exceptions, optional/discretionary items or external actions make completion ambiguous.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>A directly inspected result and an agreed completion criterion may replace a formal end-state model.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/5f.md#r-5f19b651d5acf466c1dd7859f44ac365b176162893fdfbbce68705c8f62d9cc4">Complete shared DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS · 5f19b651d5ac</a></p></dd><dt>Domain profile</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/e4.md#r-e4949107832fbe39343d97638d14c25eb3b412e21e957a49c11939f8450ef430">Complete shared DOMAIN PROFILE · e4949107832f</a></p></dd><dt>Expected engineering payoff</dt><dd><dl><dt>Claim</dt><dd><p>Avoid or expose the controlled failure: Premature closure, abandoned work, or a successful-looking case whose required effect did not occur.</p></dd><dt>Evidence qualification</dt><dd><p>A mechanism-level rationale, not a measured universal benefit. Reference-model object distinctions and executable specifications are direct evidence of conceptual/implementation semantics, not a comparative estimate of benefit.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Case owner, requester/recipient and engine completion evaluator.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>A permitted engine completion is not authority to dismiss somebody else’s unresolved obligation.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Communicate the completion claim and its exclusions, including any approved transfer of remaining work.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>No unaccounted active work remains under the chosen scope; the result claim states what was and was not established.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Premature closure, abandoned work, or a successful-looking case whose required effect did not occur.</p></li><li><p>Overly strong completion rules can keep legitimate cases open indefinitely; CMMN completion deliberately permits specified non-success terminal states.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/7c.md#r-7c1fb3953dc95bc0c2e42e9a5c662f2e1471441ef993defa72e11efdea4f38e6">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · 7c1fb3953dc9</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><p>REFINED: distinguish structural completion, case lifecycle completion and accepted external outcome.</p></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Declare what instance completion establishes, what work may remain and whether residual effects are closed, transferred or explicitly accepted; do not equate terminal status with success. Retain scoped completion accounting, not a universal rule that every child must succeed.</p></dd><dt>Ceremony vs property</dt><dd><dl><dt>Protected function</dt><dd><p>Premature closure, abandoned work, or a successful-looking case whose required effect did not occur.</p></dd><dt>Minimal adequate alternative</dt><dd><p>A directly inspected result and an agreed completion criterion may replace a formal end-state model.</p></dd><dt>Fuller form trigger</dt><dd><p>Parallel work, exceptions, optional/discretionary items or external actions make completion ambiguous.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Case owner, requester/recipient and engine completion evaluator.</p></dd><dt>Cost of simplification</dt><dd><p>Removing the mechanism is acceptable only while the cheap path still establishes this claim: No unaccounted active work remains under the chosen scope; the result claim states what was and was not established.</p></dd><dt>Retirement or omission</dt><dd><p>Retire or omit the extra machinery when the trigger, consumer or relevant dependency disappears, after settling/transferring the outstanding work or evidence that still relies on it. A directly inspected result and an agreed completion criterion may replace a formal end-state model.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Current status</dt><dd><p>RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM</p></dd><dt>Property kind</dt><dd><p>CORRECTNESS_OR_EVIDENCE_CRITERION</p></dd><dt>Evidence strength partitions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/4d.md#r-4d3d5e5316f36d4df06fa3237acffbbc0c23eb507c89a44e87083b629f8b89ce">Complete shared EVIDENCE STRENGTH PARTITIONS · 4d3d5e5316f3</a></p></dd><dt>Primary source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S004</p></li><li><p>EWM-S017</p></li><li><p>EWM-S019</p></li><li><p>EWM-S051</p></li><li><p>EWM-S053</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Critical source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S016</p></li><li><p>EWM-S041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Empirical or case source ids</dt><dd><ol></ol></dd><dt>Contrary evidence</dt><dd><dl><dt>Source criticism ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-C009</p></li><li><p>EWM-C011</p></li><li><p>EWM-C017</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S016</p></li><li><p>EWM-S041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Analytical objection</dt><dd><p>Overly strong completion rules can keep legitimate cases open indefinitely; ## EWM:EWM-005

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-008.md#EWM-EWM-005">EWM:EWM-005</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EWM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EWM-005</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Joint control, data, resource and action interpretation</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/2a.md#r-2a7fb4789ad8f8cf29faae793fc48775f7c77ca5901195bc8785a58c2cee7eeb">Complete shared origin class · 2a7fb4789ad8</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_SHARED_ANCESTRY_AND_DOCUMENTED_INTERSECTIONS</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/74.md#r-74f94fd34f04f81941bc8f1e2cd33c2888307973a0942ab9eeaaa39c166e64c1">Complete shared evidence limit · 74f94fd34f04</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Task enabling/transition predicates together with data, resource and action meaning</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A consequential task’s required input, state, resource and effect assumptions can be examined together.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Interpret an enabled task jointly with its information inputs, eligible/available actor, interaction obligations and external effect boundary.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A model is being used to justify real coordination rather than only illustrate a route.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Keep perspectives in one concise task contract when separate models would duplicate information.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/68.md#r-6856364aba0789f609eee9ff6c9e28a3c2aea1406120f8d5e684b3c364047c5f">Complete shared prerequisites required input · 6856364aba07</a></p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/dc.md#r-dc54a13410b676c6d416154273546cd7a460a52099bbd934aef3424b1db36360">Complete shared assumptions · dc54a13410b6</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Eligibility, capability and authority are separate checks; the workflow cannot invent any of them.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Provide the work item with the data, responsible party and unresolved dependencies needed to act.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Modeller and operational coordinator considering whether a proposed execution can actually proceed.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A consequential task’s required input, state, resource and effect assumptions can be examined together.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A sound route requires missing data, an unavailable person or an action the performer cannot lawfully/operatively undertake.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>A sound route requires missing data, an unavailable person or an action the performer cannot lawfully/operatively undertake.</p></li><li><p>Cross-perspective state spaces and maintenance costs grow; modelling everything can be counterproductive.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/79.md#r-7927b63e002e59f5173b355c59cdfef90b01c0d3a77eec88d4cc858d156da62e">Complete shared criticism · 7927b63e002e</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-007.md#EWM-EWM-S006">EWM:EWM-S006</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-007.md#EWM-EWM-S007">EWM:EWM-S007</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-007.md#EWM-EWM-S014">EWM:EWM-S014</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-008.md#EWM-EWM-S015">EWM:EWM-S015</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-008.md#EWM-EWM-S016">EWM:EWM-S016</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-009.md#EWM-EWM-S034">EWM:EWM-S034</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM:EWM-R004</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Keep perspectives in one concise task contract when separate models would duplicate information.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Which omitted perspective can change an actual decision in the form</dt><dd><p>Synchronising merges reconcile active branches; subsequent revisions separate structured, acyclic and general forms.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>Parallel or inclusive branching followed by convergence. The consequential failure is Waiting for a branch never activated, firing early, joining different iterations or firing more than once.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>Waiting for a branch never activated, firing early, joining different iterations or firing more than once.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Choose join semantics for the actual activation and re-entry model, specifying which branch arrivals are required, which remain possible and when the join resets.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>Parallel or inclusive branching followed by convergence.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>A structured split/join pair or explicit list of launched tasks avoids a general non-local join when the work permits it.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/c9.md#r-c9b07d6b07a401e968ec8a0b43253b4e70da0b46d3e42037d3a152326f71f8a7">Complete shared DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS · c9b07d6b07a4</a></p></dd><dt>Domain profile</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/d8.md#r-d8b2fe88e4d93e178ee035c15a025a57972e29d3a97f075a7d29c67e3bbf9f66">Complete shared DOMAIN PROFILE · d8b2fe88e4d9</a></p></dd><dt>Expected engineering payoff</dt><dd><dl><dt>Claim</dt><dd><p>Avoid or expose the controlled failure: Waiting for a branch never activated, firing early, joining different iterations or firing more than once.</p></dd><dt>Evidence qualification</dt><dd><p>A mechanism-level rationale, not a measured universal benefit. Patterns and formal workflow languages specify behaviours under their stated context assumptions. Historical feature comparisons and counterexamples do not establish the best engine for a present task.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Engine designer, workflow modeller and operator diagnosing a stuck join.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>Join firing establishes a coordination condition, not success of each outside action.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Communicate branch activation/completion/cancellation information at the level required by the selected join.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>Adversarial traces with absent, late, duplicate and re-entered branches yield the specified convergence behaviour.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Waiting for a branch never activated, firing early, joining different iterations or firing more than once.</p></li><li><p>General joins require non-local reasoning and can create circular dependencies; pattern faithfulness remains disputed in part of the literature.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/57.md#r-57d96f24760701b0b8e3c8f59aa4288c2ab833162cda62311a06f9011c9d7896">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · 57d96f247607</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><p>REFINED: the 2006 revised view splits previously conflated assumptions rather than treating one OR-join label as universal.</p></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Choose join semantics for the actual activation and re-entry model, specifying which branch arrivals are required, which remain possible and when the join resets. Retain precise context-specific synchronisation; do not claim the taxonomy settles every formalisation dispute.</p></dd><dt>Ceremony vs property</dt><dd><dl><dt>Protected function</dt><dd><p>Waiting for a branch never activated, firing early, joining different iterations or firing more than once.</p></dd><dt>Minimal adequate alternative</dt><dd><p>A structured split/join pair or explicit list of launched tasks avoids a general non-local join when the work permits it.</p></dd><dt>Fuller form trigger</dt><dd><p>Parallel or inclusive branching followed by convergence.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Engine designer, workflow modeller and operator diagnosing a stuck join.</p></dd><dt>Cost of simplification</dt><dd><p>Removing the mechanism is acceptable only while the cheap path still establishes this claim: Adversarial traces with absent, late, duplicate and re-entered branches yield the specified convergence behaviour.</p></dd><dt>Retirement or omission</dt><dd><p>Retire or omit the extra machinery when the trigger, consumer or relevant dependency disappears, after settling/transferring the outstanding work or evidence that still relies on it.
